Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on probabilities structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 499. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 998 with relations in degree 3.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
